## Project synthesis — proposed post-C5A endpoint review

[Birds-eye research synthesis](research/methodology/BFSS_PROJECT_BIRDS_EYE_SYNTHESIS_POST_C4C_2026_11_14.md) compares live receipts with the frozen internal monograph. Ground-state certification and positive-sector eigenpairs share physical symmetry/source-domain obligations but have distinct mechanisms. A **conditional fixed-positive-regulator fidelity target** may avoid requiring a closed epsilon-zero auxiliary operator or two-regulator comparison first: native bounded-reciprocal Hardy plus genuine source singlet/spinor and supercharge energy-domain identities would imply `1−F ≤ κ qε`. These remain open source obligations. Recommend reviewing this endpoint after C5A's exact verdict, with explicit consumers before further generic infrastructure. Running gate/allocation unchanged; no new qualification or staged experiment.

## Forward research during C4C — positive form comparison and zero-limit kernel control

[Research memorandum](research/spectral-p2-original-field/P3_AUX_RAD_C4C_FORWARD_RESEARCH_POSITIVE_FORM_COMPARISON_AND_ZERO_LIMIT_KERNEL_2026_11_14.md) at reviewed `63fddbbcf7de93cd1a1fba07b5616532f5fd2c0d`: native bounded ratio/PiLp graph transport could yield common positive charge domains and diagonal form comparison. Conditional form-order argument sharpens the future resolvent bound to `|ε−δ|/[a(ε+δ)]` without needing the full polarized form difference first. Inspected pinned Mathlib `holderL`/`lsmul` as bounded-action donors. Exact abstract interval countercontrol shows that a closed zero-regulator limit can acquire a new kernel despite equality of every positive-regulator kernel. Simon's decreasing-form theorem supplies an abstract-limit route, whose source realization/kernel remain separate. **Research only, no new Lean qualification or staged gate; C4C remains the active strict run.**

D1 stop remains in force, full TD-C1A strict R4 untouched, M5BF0/G0B2 accepted results unchanged.\n\n## Compulsory start/finish protocol

**Start:** read the JSON checkpoint, the current source and independent consumer, their pinned workflow, the last qualified acceptance receipt, and relevant red ledger. For any physical source, domain, coercivity or unification gate, consult the **pinned Edition 0.7 monograph crosswalk below** at its explicit source SHA; do not silently import its older snapshot statuses. Compare the proposed gate to M1–M8 below and the historical [P3 spectral bridge](research/spectral-p2-original-field/P3_OPERATOR_COLUMNS_PHYSICAL_SPECTRAL_BRIDGE_REASSESSMENT_2026_11_12.md). Distinguish DIRECT REUSE, EXACT SOURCE ADAPTER, METHOD ONLY and FAILED/UNQUALIFIED. Check source Matlib version and original Fock types before writing.

**Before a new gate:** name its milestone, exactly what theorem will be proved, the source carrier, prior qualified theorems it consumes, independent consumer, exact workflow/manifest pins and what is **not** proved. Do not run a convenient unrelated green merely to increase counts. Do not touch `main`.

**Every RED/GREEN:** verify exact run conclusion and tested SHA independently. On RED identify first causal failure, preserve artifact/log/negative knowledge, and repair narrowly (generic-first before resource escalation). On GREEN promote **only** named declarations after source, consumer, cold build and axiom audit. **Commit an updated [live checkpoint](BFSS_GATE_STATE.json) with verdict and next decision BEFORE beginning a different gate.** If a run is pending, do not infer its conclusion.

**Formal review trigger:** after two analogous reds, every five accepted gates, each milestone transition, any new counterexample, or the user's request for the big picture. Reassess dependencies, prune redundant work, retain failures, keep explicit blockers. Never promise a spectral result from an algebraic or Hilbert-space result.

The user's shortest reliable resume instruction is:
> **Resume BFSS from `BFSS_ACTIVE_ROADMAP.md` and `BFSS_GATE_STATE.json` on `integration/bfss-unified-research-20261108`; verify the latest CI receipt first.**

## Standing scientific-method experimental protocol (mandatory before new Python work)

**Follow the owner's eight-stage cycle:** **observe → question → background research → falsifiable hypothesis → controlled experiment → analysis → conclusion → communication**. Every computational research question receives a unique registered test in [the scientific method protocol](research/methodology/BFSS_SCIENTIFIC_METHOD_PROTOCOL.md) and [the experiment registry](research/methodology/BFSS_EXPERIMENT_REGISTRY.json) **before its prospective execution**, with exact source pins, controls, falsifiers and explicit decision consequences. Earlier exploratory results are **not** retroactively preregistered. Exact integer counterexamples and float tolerances have distinct evidential status; repeated random passes may justify a Lean theorem target, never a universal/source-physical proof.

**Current first test:** `E-M7-001-R1` was preregistered and survived its finite tests after a preserved causal red and bounded symbolic repair (#43335288061 red, #43335892084 green). This does not promote the unnormalized source-field universal bound, gauge-fixed coercivity, positive eigenvalues or three generations. M1 B4A is now qualified and **B4B** is the next proof gate.

The historical pinned source coefficient audits and the new reproducible [sharp-constant audit](research/spectral-p2-original-field/tools/audit_source_fermion_sharp_constant.py) suggest a higher-payoff **early M7 source-field lemma**, independent of M1 proof order. The [exact research receipt and proof blueprint](research/spectral-p2-original-field/P3_SOURCE_FERMION_SHARP_CONSTANT_EXACT_NUMERICAL_RESEARCH_2026_11_12.md) identify the source 48×48 skew matrix `K0(X)`, a candidate universal polynomial identity and a numerically supported spectral reduction. The induced **conditional** original unnormalized Fock-field constant is `16√(2/3)≈13.06394529`, improving conditional `8√3≈13.85640646` by `5.72%`, saturated at equal nonzero spatial/color singular values. Rank-one valley ratio is `8√2≈11.31370850`, but that is **not** a global bound or an accepted physical form estimate.

- **New explicit fork for M7:** prove exact signed source `B(X)` equality with `K0`; prove original 48×48 matrix polynomial with **multiplicity** control, not merely 16 successful exact-integer samples; prove the original CAR norm formula; only then attempt the sharp `16√(2/3)` Lean operator norm theorem. The 12×12 reduced `Cl(3)` symbolic characteristic calculation is a proof-search donor only.

No source reducing sector, fixed-color slicing, or closed-form coercivity is yet certified.
- **Decision:** continue **D5-B4A** next. Revisit the M7 route only after an independent exact-source certificate or a physical counterexample. Numerical optimization may prioritize theorem statements; it cannot replace original-source typing, domain control or Lean qualification.

## Alternative independent spectral decision track — audit before adopting

[Chapter 14](https://github.com/etblink/math/tree/b603f4c1715e15f5f92c69d7fb071992dbaa9f40/contributions/bfss-kotler/internal-monograph/chapters/ch14_frontier.tex) discusses a **different**, externally reported supersymmetry-multiplet route (Bajaj–Lin, and separately Michel–Scheinpflug–Yin). It is **not** our high-`λ₂` proof and **not** qualified on our original source. Before treating it as a competing critical path, independently audit: original physical all-charge anticommutators (including Gauss removal), common closed domains, `Q_αH^{-1/2}` **only on a legitimate positive-energy spectral subspace**, nonzero `84` / `128` superspin projector, scattering-channel exclusion, exact original `H=(1/16)T†T` normalization, and normalizability of proposed states. These `84/128` are **superspin** components of a positive-energy multiplet, **not particle generations**. The external claims are unverified here. Schedule a read-only go/no-go comparison at the next M5/M6 review or earlier if a sourced counterexample changes the main route; **do not interrupt B4A or silently claim external spectral existence**.

## Exploratory unification remains off the spectral qualification path

Chapters 15–17 impose **negative controls**, not a second proof-critical task queue: the SU(2) adjoint color three is not three invariant generation projectors; `120` gauge-invariant skew-bilinear *coefficients* are not conserved physical observables; the naive labelled `P_+(n)` Hopf eigenspace fails gauge descent under antipodal exchange; the line-twisted `RP^8` gamma section is not a chiral family index; spatial Spin(9) cannot be declared internal Spin(10) GUT representation without an actual physical map. Any NFC/Hopf/category/three-family proposal must show a source-natural, gauge-equivariant operator/section, dynamics, closed-domain compatibility, and an independently testable observable. The three rank-12 arithmetic sectors stay exploratory, **not** three-generation proof.
